<script>
/* 最新YQN只读快照独立接入；主表权威值不被外仓覆盖，对账原因分证据等级。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.freshYqnModel?.ready) return;
  const packet = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('freshYqnData').textContent);
  if (!packet.meta.completePagination) throw Error('YQN来源未完成全部分页');
  const bySku = new Map(packet.rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => cxEsc(String(value ?? ''));
  const qty = value => finite(value) ? cxQty(value) : '待核';
  const signed = value => finite(value) ? (value > 0 ? '+' : '') + cxQty(value) : '待核';
  const date = packet.meta.queryDateShanghai, dateLabel = date + ' API查询';
  const labels = {CA:'美西 CA', GA:'美东 GA', NJ:'美东北 NJ'};
  const states = {matched:'可用及预留一致', explained:'可用差额已有依据', partly_explained:'部分解释，仍待核', unresolved:'差异或缺项待核'};
  const certainty = {confirmed:'已核事实', possible:'可能因素', pending:'待核'};
  const state = {scope:'active', region:'', status:'difference', cause:'', search:'', page:0};
  const masterOverride = new Map(); let masterMeta = null, hasMasterOverride = false;
  const rawGroups = record => record?.warehouseGroups || [];
  function regionOf(warehouse) { return /_(CA|GA|NJ)(?:_|$)/.exec(String(warehouse))?.[1] || 'unknown'; }
  function mainFor(record, region = '') {
    const injected = masterOverride.get(record.sku);
    const current = injected || (hasMasterOverride ? {masterRegions:{},available:null,reserved:null,transit:null} : {...record.master,masterRegions:record.masterRegions});
    const regions = current?.masterRegions || record.masterRegions || {};
    const values = region ? regions[region] || {} : current || record.master || {};
    return {available:finite(values.available)?values.available:null, reserved:finite(values.reserved)?values.reserved:null,
      transit:finite(values.transit)?values.transit:null, regions,
      sourceDate:masterMeta?.sourceSnapshotDate || record.masterFileDate,
      sourceHash:masterMeta?.primarySource?.sha256 || packet.meta.masterSource.sha256};
  }
  function comparison(record, region = state.region) {
    const main = mainFor(record, region), yqn = region ? record.yqnRegional?.[region] || {} : {
      available:record.yqnAvailable, reserved:record.yqnReserved, physical:record.yqnPhysical, transit:record.yqnTransit};
    const difference = finite(main.available) && finite(yqn.available) ? main.available-yqn.available : null;
    const reservedDifference = finite(main.reserved) && finite(yqn.reserved) ? main.reserved-yqn.reserved : null;
    let reasons = (record.reconciliation?.reasons || []).filter(reason => !['available_matched','reserved_difference','unresolved_difference'].includes(reason.class));
    const proofSame = !region && main.sourceHash === packet.meta.masterSource.sha256
      && main.available === record.master.available && main.reserved === record.master.reserved;
    if (!proofSame) reasons = reasons.map(reason => reason.class === 'own_warehouse_scope' && reason.certainty === 'confirmed'
      ? {...reason, certainty:'possible', text:reason.text + ' 当前主表作用域已变化，需按新源重新闭合。' } : reason);
    let status = 'unresolved';
    if (difference === 0 && reservedDifference === 0) {
      status='matched'; reasons=[{class:'available_matched',certainty:'confirmed',text:'同作用域可用及预留完整套均一致。'},...reasons.filter(r=>!['cutoff_difference','main_missing','yqn_no_exact_record'].includes(r.class))];
    } else if (difference === 0) {
      reasons.unshift({class:'available_matched',certainty:'confirmed',text:'可用完整套一致。' });
    } else if (proofSame && record.reconciliation?.status === 'explained') status='explained';
    else if(proofSame && record.reconciliation?.status === 'partly_explained')status='partly_explained';
    if (reservedDifference !== 0) {const bridge=proofSame&&record.shipmentReservationBridge;reasons.push({class:'reserved_difference',certainty:'pending',text:bridge?'原预留差 '+signed(reservedDifference)+'套，其中'+qty(bridge.masterReservedRowsWithCompleteYqnShipment)+'个主表登记已有完整销售出库证据；按已证出库登记扣除后，剩余预留状态差 '+signed(bridge.remainingReservedDifference)+'套待核。':finite(reservedDifference)?'预留差额未取得逐订单核销依据。差额 '+signed(reservedDifference)+'套。':'至少一方预留来源未齐。'});}
    if (status === 'explained' && reservedDifference !== 0) status='partly_explained';
    if (difference !== 0 && status !== 'explained') reasons.push({class:'unresolved_difference',certainty:'pending',text:finite(difference)?'可用差额 '+signed(difference)+'套；未用较大值覆盖另一来源。':'至少一方可用来源未齐；未知未计0。'});
    return {record, main, yqn, difference, reservedDifference, reasons, status};
  }
  function applyYqn(target, record) {
    if (!target) return;
    if (!target.historicalYqnSnapshot) target.historicalYqnSnapshot = {
      date:target.ageDate, ageBands:target.ageBands, warehouseGroups:target.warehouseGroups};
    if (!record) {
      Object.assign(target,{warehouseGroups:[],ageBands:{},ageSets:null,age:'本次完整YQN来源未匹配此精确身份，当前库龄待核',ageDate:dateLabel+' · 未匹配',yqnMatched:false,yqnAvailable:null,yqnReserved:null,yqnPhysical:null,yqn_available_sets:null,yqn_reserved_sets:null,yqn_physical_sets:null,yqnUnsalable:null,currentYqnEvidence:null,yqnSourceDate:dateLabel,ownWarehouseBatches:[],ownWarehouseSummary:null});
      return;
    }
    target.warehouseGroups = rawGroups(record);
    target.ageBands = record.ageBands || {}; target.ageSets = record.ageSetsLower ?? record.ageSets;
    target.age = Object.entries(record.ageBands || {}).filter(([,n])=>n>0).map(([band,n])=>(packet.meta.ageUiBandLabels?.[band] || band)+' '+qty(n)+'套').join('、') || (record.ageBands ? '当前API各龄段已证齐套下界0' : '当前API库龄或齐套依据待核');
    target.ageDate=dateLabel; target.ageBandsComplete=record.ageBandsComplete; target.yqnMatched=record.yqnMatched;
    target.yqnAvailable=target.yqn_available_sets=record.yqnAvailable; target.yqnReserved=target.yqn_reserved_sets=record.yqnReserved;
    target.yqnPhysical=target.yqn_physical_sets=record.yqnPhysical;target.yqnUnsalable=record.unsalablePhysical;
    target.currentYqnEvidence=record; target.yqnSourceDate=dateLabel;
    target.ownWarehouseBatches=(record.ownWarehouseCurrentBatches||[]).map(b=>({...b,complete_sets:Array.from({length:b.parts},(_,i)=>i+1).every(p=>finite(b.components?.[p]))?b.completeSets:null,complete_sets_lower_bound:b.completeSets,pack_parts:b.parts,pack_version:'原主表未分版本',part_quantities:b.components,date:record.masterFileDate+'主表当前明细'}));
    target.ownWarehouseSummary={available:null,reserved:null,date:record.masterFileDate,knownCurrentGroups:target.ownWarehouseBatches.length,meaning:'仅本次云主表明确自有仓在仓行；无当前分组不回用历史正量，也不据缺行推定0'};
    target.yqnInventorySource={queryDate:date,updatedAtMaxRaw:packet.meta.inventoryUpdatedMaxRaw,sha256:packet.meta.sourceFiles['inventory_records.json'].sha256};
    target.inventoryIssues=[...(target.inventoryIssues||[]).filter(s=>!String(s).includes('YQN11/12')),'YQN已读取'+date+'最新API；主表与外仓独立对账，库龄档位边界及分桶齐套下界见来源说明。'];
  }
  const touched = new Set();
  for (const map of [typeof cxStockMap !== 'undefined' && cxStockMap,typeof sqStockBySku !== 'undefined' && sqStockBySku]) if (map)
    for (const [sku, target] of map) {if (!touched.has(target)) {applyYqn(target, bySku.get(sku));touched.add(target);}}
  if (typeof sqStockRecord === 'function') {const before=sqStockRecord;sqStockRecord=function(sku){const row=before.apply(this,arguments);applyYqn(row,bySku.get(sku));return row;};}
  function makeWarehouses(record, previous = []) {
    const grouped=new Map();
    for (const g of rawGroups(record)) {
      if (g.inventory_type!=='Sellable') continue;
      let item=grouped.get(g.warehouse_code);
      if(!item){item={key:g.warehouse_code,code:g.warehouse_code,label:g.warehouse_code,type:'external',region:regionOf(g.warehouse),date:dateLabel,groups:[]};grouped.set(g.warehouse_code,item);}
      item.groups.push(g);
    }
    for(const w of grouped.values()){
      w.inventoryRowPresent=w.groups.some(g=>g.inventory_source_rows.length);
      for(const [field,source]of [['available','available_complete_sets'],['reserved','reserved_complete_sets'],['physical','physical_complete_sets'],['transit','in_transit_complete_sets']]){
        const vs=w.groups.map(g=>g[source]);w[field]=vs.length&&vs.every(finite)?vs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0):null;
        w[field+'Observed']=w.groups.reduce((a,g)=>a+(finite(g[source+'_lower_bound'])?g[source+'_lower_bound']:0),0);
      }
      w.earliestInbound=w.groups.map(g=>g.earliestInbound).filter(Boolean).sort()[0]||null;
      w.latestInbound=w.groups.map(g=>g.latestInbound).filter(Boolean).sort().at(-1)||null;
    }
    // 自有仓不是YQN，保留独立来源；只有当前主表明确自有仓在仓行才补当前数量。
    const ownGroups=new Map();
    for(const b of record.ownWarehouseCurrentBatches||[]){const region=b.region,key='OWN_'+region;let w=ownGroups.get(key);if(!w){w={key,code:key,label:'自有仓库',type:'own',region,date:record.masterFileDate+'主表明细',available:0,availableObserved:0,reserved:null,reservedObserved:null,physical:null,transit:null,batches:[]};ownGroups.set(key,w);}const complete=Array.from({length:b.parts},(_,i)=>i+1).every(p=>finite(b.components?.[p]));w.available=complete&&finite(w.available)?w.available+b.completeSets:null;w.availableObserved+=b.completeSets;w.batches.push(b);}
    return [...grouped.values(),...ownGroups.values()];
  }
  if(typeof rwData!=='undefined'){
    rwData.meta.externalDate=dateLabel;rwData.meta.ageDate=dateLabel;
    rwData.meta.warehouseMeaning='YQN API最新全页快照；同仓同版本配套，残箱与不可售单列；不加到主表。自有仓保持其主表明细日期。';
    for(const row of rwData.rows){const record=bySku.get(row.sku);applyYqn(row,record);row.warehouses=record?makeWarehouses(record):[];}
  }
  // 当前监控库龄同步；原降价策略依据单独保留，不据新库存重批降价或改执行量。
  if(root.markdownFollowupModel?.rows){for(const row of root.markdownFollowupModel.rows){if(!row.strategyAgeText)row.strategyAgeText=row.ageText;row.strategyAgeDate=row.strategyAgeDate||'原11/16策略快照';const record=bySku.get(row.sku),stock={};applyYqn(stock,record);row.ageText=stock.age;row.ageDate=stock.ageDate;row.currentYqnEvidence=record||null;}root.markdownFollowupModel.meta.ageDate=dateLabel;}
  if(typeof nmData!=='undefined')for(const row of nmData.rows||[]){const record=bySku.get(row.inventorySku||row.sku);row.currentYqnEvidence=record||null;row.currentAgeDate=dateLabel;row.currentAgeBands=record?.ageBands||{};}
  function reasonText(reasons){return reasons.map(r=>'【'+certainty[r.certainty]+'】'+r.text).join('；');}
  function chooseRows() {
    const search=state.search.trim().toLowerCase();
    return packet.rows.filter(r=>(state.scope==='all'||r.active)&&(!search||r.sku.toLowerCase().includes(search)))
      .map(r=>comparison(r)).filter(v=>{
        if(state.status==='difference'&&v.status==='matched')return false;
        if(state.status==='positive'&&!(v.difference>0))return false;
        if(state.status==='negative'&&!(v.difference<0))return false;
        if(state.status==='missing_main'&&finite(v.main.available))return false;
        if(state.status==='missing_yqn'&&finite(v.yqn.available))return false;
        if(state.status==='api_async'&&!(finite(v.record.apiInventoryAgeDifference)&&v.record.apiInventoryAgeDifference!==0))return false;
        if(['matched','explained','partly_explained','unresolved'].includes(state.status)&&v.status!==state.status)return false;
        return !state.cau
